  In this paper, we take a semi-parametric approach for calibrating confidence in estimates of the $p$th powers of Shapley additive explanations. We show that, by treating the SHAP curve as a nuisance function that must be estimated from data, one can reliably construct asymptotically normal estimates of the $p$th powers of SHAP. When $p \geq 2$, we show a de-biased estimator that combines U-statistics with Neyman orthogonal scores for functionals of nested regressions is asymptotically normal. When $1 \leq p < 2$ (and the hence target parameter is not twice differentiable), we construct de-biased U-statistics for a smoothed alternative. In particular, we show how to carefully tune the temperature parameter of the smoothing function in order to obtain inference for the true, unsmoothed $p$th power. We complement these results by presenting a Neyman orthogonal loss that can be used to learn the SHAP curve via empirical risk minimization and discussing excess risk guarantees for commonly used function classes.

</details>


### [112] [Deep Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10587)
*Jinyuan Chang,Yuling Jiao,Lican Kang,Junjie Shi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于条件扩散模型的深度自举框架用于非参数回归，通过生成响应变量样本来实现条件样本均值估计，相比传统方法具有统一的生成框架和理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自举方法将条件分布估计、采样和非参数回归解耦处理，缺乏统一框架。需要一种能够处理高维或多模态分布的高效采样方法，同时保证非参数估计的准确性。

Method: 构建条件扩散模型学习给定协变量时响应变量的分布，使用该模型生成自举样本（原始协变量配对新合成的响应），将非参数回归重新表述为条件样本均值估计，通过学习的条件扩散模型直接实现。

Result: 建立了所提方法的严格理论保证，推导了学习条件分布与目标条件分布之间Wasserstein距离的最优端到端收敛率，进一步建立了自举过程的收敛保证。数值研究证明了该方法在复杂回归任务中的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出的深度自举框架通过条件扩散模型统一了条件分布学习、采样和非参数回归，利用扩散模型的表达能力实现了高效采样和准确估计，为复杂回归任务提供了理论保证的有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a novel deep bootstrap framework for nonparametric regression based on conditional diffusion models. Specifically, we construct a conditional diffusion model to learn the distribution of the response variable given the covariates. This model is then used to generate bootstrap samples by pairing the original covariates with newly synthesized responses. We reformulate nonparametric regression as conditional sample mean estimation, which is implemented directly via the learned conditional diffusion model. Unlike traditional bootstrap methods that decouple the estimation of the conditional distribution, sampling, and nonparametric regression, our approach integrates these components into a unified generative framework. With the expressive capacity of diffusion models, our method facilitates both efficient sampling from high-dimensional or multimodal distributions and accurate nonparametric estimation. We establish rigorous theoretical guarantees for the proposed method. In particular, we derive optimal end-to-end convergence rates in the Wasserstein distance between the learned and target conditional distributions. Building on this foundation, we further establish the convergence guarantees of the resulting bootstrap procedure. Numerical studies demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of our approach for complex regression tasks.

</details>


### [113] [Bayesian Inference of Contextual Bandit Policies via Empirical Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10608)
*Jiangrong Ouyang,Mingming Gong,Howard Bondell*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于经验似然的贝叶斯推断方法，用于在有限样本条件下对多个上下文赌博机策略进行联合分析，该方法对小样本具有鲁棒性，并能提供准确的策略价值评估不确定性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文赌博机问题中，策略推断起着关键作用。现有方法在有限样本条件下进行策略分析和比较时，往往缺乏准确的不确定性量化能力，特别是在小样本情况下表现不佳。

Method: 使用经验似然开发贝叶斯推断方法，对多个上下文赌博机策略进行联合分析。该方法能够处理有限样本情况，提供策略价值评估的准确不确定性测量，并支持灵活的策略比较推断。

Result: 通过蒙特卡洛模拟证明了所提推断方法的有效性，并在青少年身体质量指数数据集上进行了应用验证。结果表明该方法对小样本具有鲁棒性，能够提供准确的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 提出的基于经验似然的贝叶斯推断方法为上下文赌博机策略分析提供了一种有效的工具，特别适用于有限样本条件下的策略价值评估和比较，具有鲁棒性和准确的不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: Policy inference plays an essential role in the contextual bandit problem. In this paper, we use empirical likelihood to develop a Bayesian inference method for the joint analysis of multiple contextual bandit policies in finite sample regimes. The proposed inference method is robust to small sample sizes and is able to provide accurate uncertainty measurements for policy value evaluation. In addition, it allows for flexible inferences on policy comparison with full uncertainty quantification. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed inference method using Monte Carlo simulations and its application to an adolescent body mass index data set.

</details>


### [114] [Highly Adaptive Principal Component Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10613)
*Mingxun Wang,Alejandro Schuler,Mark van der Laan,Carlos García Meixide*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于主成分的HAL和HAR方法(PCHAL/PCHAR)，通过无监督降维提高计算效率，同时保持原方法的性能优势，并揭示了HAL/HAR Gram算子的谱结构与离散正弦基的傅里叶联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAL方法在最小平滑性假设下能实现几乎维度无关的收敛率，但在高维情况下由于需要构建大型基矩阵导致计算成本过高。HAR作为可扩展替代方案被提出，但仍需进一步优化计算效率。

Method: 提出PCHAL和PCHAR方法，通过主成分分析对HAL/HAR的基矩阵进行降维处理，实现结果无关的维度约简。该方法基于HAL/HAR Gram算子的谱分析，利用其与离散正弦基的傅里叶结构联系。

Result: PCHAL和PCHAR在计算效率上获得显著提升，同时保持了与原始HAL和HAR相当的实证性能。揭示了HAL/HAR Gram算子的主成分与离散正弦基之间的谱联系，表明PC截断具有明确的傅里叶型结构。

Conclusion: PCHAL和PCHAR方法通过主成分降维有效解决了HAL/HAR在高维场景下的计算瓶颈，在保持统计性能的同时大幅提升计算效率，并揭示了这些方法的底层傅里叶结构特性。

Abstract: The Highly Adaptive Lasso (HAL) is a nonparametric regression method that achieves almost dimension-free convergence rates under minimal smoothness assumptions, but its implementation can be computationally prohibitive in high dimensions due to the large basis matrix it requires. The Highly Adaptive Ridge (HAR) has been proposed as a scalable alternative. Building on both procedures, we introduce the Principal Component based Highly Adaptive Lasso (PCHAL) and Principal Component based Highly Adaptive Ridge (PCHAR). These estimators constitute an outcome-blind dimension reduction which offer substantial gains in computational efficiency and match the empirical performances of HAL and HAR. We also uncover a striking spectral link between the leading principal components of the HAL/HAR Gram operator and a discrete sinusoidal basis, revealing an explicit Fourier-type structure underlying the PC truncation.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Kemeny Medians: Consensus Ranking Distributions Definition, Properties and Statistical Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10640)
*Stephan Clémençon,Ekhine Irurozki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于局部排序中位数的新方法，通过共识排序分布（CRD）来近似排序分布，使用Kendall τ距离作为成本函数，并开发了高效的树状结构学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越经典的共识和中位数理论，为对称群上的概率分布提供新的摘要方法。由于对称群缺乏向量空间结构，需要开发不依赖该结构的高效学习方法。

Method: 引入局部排序中位数概念，提出共识排序分布（CRD）作为稀疏混合模型。使用Kendall τ距离作为成本函数，证明最优失真可表示为成对概率函数。开发了自上而下的树状结构统计算法，从Kemeny中位数的狄拉克质量开始，逐步细化到经验排序数据分布。

Result: 理论证明使用Kendall τ距离时，最优失真可表达为成对概率函数，为高效算法开发奠定基础。提出的树状算法能够渐进细化CRD，数值实验支持了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为排序分布摘要提供了新框架，克服了对称群缺乏向量空间结构的限制，提出的算法在理论和实证上均表现出良好性能。

Abstract: In this article we develop a new method for summarizing a ranking distribution, \textit{i.e.} a probability distribution on the symmetric group $\mathfrak{S}_n$, beyond the classical theory of consensus and Kemeny medians. Based on the notion of \textit{local ranking median}, we introduce the concept of \textit{consensus ranking distribution} ($\crd$), a sparse mixture model of Dirac masses on $\mathfrak{S}_n$, in order to approximate a ranking distribution with small distortion from a mass transportation perspective. We prove that by choosing the popular Kendall $τ$ distance as the cost function, the optimal distortion can be expressed as a function of pairwise probabilities, paving the way for the development of efficient learning methods that do not suffer from the lack of vector space structure on $\mathfrak{S}_n$. In particular, we propose a top-down tree-structured statistical algorithm that allows for the progressive refinement of a CRD based on ranking data, from the Dirac mass at a Kemeny median at the root of the tree to the empirical ranking data distribution itself at the end of the tree's exhaustive growth. In addition to the theoretical arguments developed, the relevance of the algorithm is empirically supported by various numerical experiments.

</details>


### [116] [Robust Assortment Optimization from Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10696)
*Miao Lu,Yuxuan Han,Han Zhong,Zhengyuan Zhou,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个考虑顾客选择行为分布偏移的鲁棒性商品组合优化框架，在已知名义模型时计算可处理，在数据驱动场景下设计统计最优算法，建立了样本复杂度的上下界并识别出"鲁棒逐项覆盖"作为最小数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的商品组合优化方法通常假设顾客偏好稳定且选择模型正确，但这些假设在现实场景中经常失效（偏好偏移和模型误设），导致泛化能力差和收入损失。需要开发能够处理顾客选择行为分布偏移的鲁棒框架。

Method: 提出鲁棒性商品组合优化框架，从生成数据的名义选择模型出发，建模潜在偏好偏移，最大化最坏情况期望收入。首先在已知名义模型时证明鲁棒组合规划的计算可处理性，然后在数据驱动场景下设计统计最优算法，最小化数据需求同时保持鲁棒性。

Result: 理论分析提供了样本复杂度的上界和匹配下界，为鲁棒泛化提供理论保证。识别出"鲁棒逐项覆盖"概念作为实现样本高效鲁棒组合学习的最小数据需求。在鲁棒性和统计效率之间建立桥梁。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了组合学习中鲁棒性和统计效率之间的空白，为不确定性下的可靠商品组合优化提供了新见解和工具。提出的框架能够处理现实世界中顾客偏好偏移和模型误设问题，提高数据驱动组合优化的可靠性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Assortment optimization is a fundamental challenge in modern retail and recommendation systems, where the goal is to select a subset of products that maximizes expected revenue under complex customer choice behaviors. While recent advances in data-driven methods have leveraged historical data to learn and optimize assortments, these approaches typically rely on strong assumptions -- namely, the stability of customer preferences and the correctness of the underlying choice models. However, such assumptions frequently break in real-world scenarios due to preference shifts and model misspecification, leading to poor generalization and revenue loss. Motivated by this limitation, we propose a robust framework for data-driven assortment optimization that accounts for potential distributional shifts in customer choice behavior. Our approach models potential preference shift from a nominal choice model that generates data and seeks to maximize worst-case expected revenue. We first establish the computational tractability of robust assortment planning when the nominal model is known, then advance to the data-driven setting, where we design statistically optimal algorithms that minimize the data requirements while maintaining robustness. Our theoretical analysis provides both upper bounds and matching lower bounds on the sample complexity, offering theoretical guarantees for robust generalization. Notably, we uncover and identify the notion of ``robust item-wise coverage'' as the minimal data requirement to enable sample-efficient robust assortment learning. Our work bridges the gap between robustness and statistical efficiency in assortment learning, contributing new insights and tools for reliable assortment optimization under uncertainty.

</details>


### [117] [Convergence Rates for Distribution Matching with Sliced Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10691)
*Gauthier Thurin,Claire Boyer,Kimia Nadjahi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于切片最优传输的分布匹配迭代方法——切片匹配方案，建立了Sliced-Wasserstein目标的Łojasiewicz型不等式，证明了高斯分布情况下的收敛性并给出了定量非渐近收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究切片匹配方案的收敛性，为基于切片最优传输的高效分布匹配方法提供理论保证，特别是在高维情况下的收敛行为分析。

Method: 采用切片匹配方案作为迭代方法，建立Sliced-Wasserstein目标的Łojasiewicz型不等式，通过控制轨迹上的常数来分析收敛性，特别针对高斯分布情况，在每次迭代中使用随机正交基进行匹配以控制特征值。

Result: 证明了切片匹配方案对高斯分布的收敛性，推导了定量非渐近收敛率，展示了维度、步长对收敛的影响，以及正交基采样的稳定效应，并通过数值实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 切片匹配方案是一种有效的分布匹配方法，对于高斯分布具有理论保证的收敛性，随机正交基采样策略能够稳定收敛过程，为高维分布匹配提供了可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the slice-matching scheme, an efficient iterative method for distribution matching based on sliced optimal transport. We investigate convergence to the target distribution and derive quantitative non-asymptotic rates. To this end, we establish __ojasiewicz-type inequalities for the Sliced-Wasserstein objective. A key challenge is to control along the trajectory the constants in these inequalities. We show that this becomes tractable for Gaussian distributions. Specifically, eigenvalues are controlled when matching along random orthonormal bases at each iteration. We complement our theory with numerical experiments and illustrate the predicted dependence on dimension and step-size, as well as the stabilizing effect of orthonormal-basis sampling.

</details>


### [118] [Deep Learning of Compositional Targets with Hierarchical Spectral Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10867)
*Hugo Tabanelli,Yatin Dandi,Luca Pesce,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 深度学习方法相比浅层方法在计算优势上的理论分析，通过高斯设置下的组合目标函数研究，发现三层模型通过分层学习比两层模型需要更少的样本复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为什么深度学习方法比浅层方法具有真正的计算优势，这是学习理论中的核心开放问题。研究在受控的高维高斯设置下，针对组合目标函数的可学习性。

Method: 使用显式的三层拟合模型，通过分层谱估计器进行训练。分析组合目标函数的可学习性，利用高斯普适性原理，研究分层学习策略。

Result: 发现三层学习策略相比两层策略在样本复杂度上存在显著分离。组合结构允许分阶段学习，中间表示揭示了输入层面无法访问的结构，将学习简化为更简单的谱估计问题。

Conclusion: 深度模型的组合结构使其能够通过分层学习策略获得计算优势，而浅层估计器必须同时解决所有组件，这解释了深度方法相比浅层方法的真正优势。

Abstract: Why depth yields a genuine computational advantage over shallow methods remains a central open question in learning theory. We study this question in a controlled high-dimensional Gaussian setting, focusing on compositional target functions. We analyze their learnability using an explicit three-layer fitting model trained via layer-wise spectral estimators. Although the target is globally a high-degree polynomial, its compositional structure allows learning to proceed in stages: an intermediate representation reveals structure that is inaccessible at the input level. This reduces learning to simpler spectral estimation problems, well studied in the context of multi-index models, whereas any shallow estimator must resolve all components simultaneously. Our analysis relies on Gaussian universality, leading to sharp separations in sample complexity between two and three-layer learning strategies.

</details>


### [119] [A Gibbs posterior sampler for inverse problem based on prior diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11059)
*Jean-François Giovannelli*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种吉布斯采样算法，用于解决线性观测系统加噪声、贝叶斯正则化且先验由扩散过程建模的逆问题中的后验采样难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在逆问题中，当观测系统为线性变换加噪声、采用贝叶斯框架正则化且先验由基于大量样本的扩散过程建模时，后验采样是一个棘手问题，需要有效的解决方案。

Method: 引入吉布斯采样算法，该方法在此类问题中尚未被探索，具有简单有效的特点，并在特定情况下提供收敛保证。

Result: 数值模拟结果明确证实了该方法的有效性，表明吉布斯采样算法在此类逆问题中特别有效且实现简单。

Conclusion: 吉布斯采样算法为解决线性观测系统加噪声、贝叶斯正则化且先验由扩散过程建模的逆问题中的后验采样问题提供了一种新颖、有效且简单的方法，具有明确的收敛保证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the issue of inversion in cases where (1) the observation system is modeled by a linear transformation and additive noise, (2) the problem is ill-posed and regularization is introduced in a Bayesian framework by an a prior density, and (3) the latter is modeled by a diffusion process adjusted on an available large set of examples. In this context, it is known that the issue of posterior sampling is a thorny one. This paper introduces a Gibbs algorithm. It appears that this avenue has not been explored, and we show that this approach is particularly effective and remarkably simple. In addition, it offers a guarantee of convergence in a clearly identified situation. The results are clearly confirmed by numerical simulations.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Understanding the Effects of AI-Assisted Critical Thinking on Human-AI Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10222)
*Harry Yizhou Tian,Hasan Amin,Ming Yin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出AI辅助批判性思维框架，通过AI分析人类决策理由并鼓励反思，在房价预测案例中显示能减少对AI的过度依赖但增加认知负荷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-AI团队决策表现常不理想，部分原因是缺乏对人类自身推理的充分检验，需要设计能直接分析人类决策理由并鼓励批判性反思的AI系统

Method: 提出AI辅助批判性思维框架，利用领域特定AI模型对人类决策进行反事实分析，帮助决策者识别决策论证中的潜在缺陷并支持修正

Result: 在房价预测案例研究中，AACT框架优于传统AI决策支持系统，能减少对AI的过度依赖，但会触发更高的认知负荷；亚组分析显示对熟悉AI技术的决策者特别有益

Conclusion: 讨论了研究结果的实际意义、AACT的使用场景和设计选择，以及使用AI促进批判性思维的考虑因素

Abstract: Despite the growing prevalence of human-AI decision making, the human-AI team's decision performance often remains suboptimal, partially due to insufficient examination of humans' own reasoning. In this paper, we explore designing AI systems that directly analyze humans' decision rationales and encourage critical reflection of their own decisions. We introduce the AI-Assisted Critical Thinking (AACT) framework, which leverages a domain-specific AI model's counterfactual analysis of human decision to help decision-makers identify potential flaws in their decision argument and support the correction of them. Through a case study on house price prediction, we find that AACT outperforms traditional AI-based decision-support in reducing over-reliance on AI, though also triggering higher cognitive load. Subgroup analysis reveals AACT can be particularly beneficial for some decision-makers such as those very familiar with AI technologies. We conclude by discussing the practical implications of our findings, use cases and design choices of AACT, and considerations for using AI to facilitate critical thinking.

</details>


### [121] [Reimagining Sign Language Technologies: Analyzing Translation Work of Chinese Deaf Online Content Creators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10235)
*Xinru Tang,Anne Marie Piper*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过访谈13位中国聋人内容创作者，揭示了手语翻译的复杂性，并基于聋人主导的翻译实践，提出利用(跨)语言实践概念重新构想手语翻译系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语翻译系统虽然承诺增强聋人获取信息和沟通的能力，但常因存在误传和简化手语沟通丰富性的风险而受到聋人群体的质疑。研究旨在通过实证证据揭示聋人沟通中翻译工作的复杂性。

Method: 通过对13位在视频分享平台上积极创作和分享手语内容的中国聋人内容创作者进行访谈，研究这一独特群体如何为聋人和听人观众制作内容。

Result: 研究发现聋人创作者在创作内容时考虑多语言和多文化因素，支持跨语言和文化的意义构建，并在翻译工作中处理相关的政治问题。这些实践展现了手语翻译的细微差别。

Conclusion: 基于这些聋人主导的翻译实践，研究运用社会语言学中的(跨)语言实践概念，重新概念化和重新构想手语翻译系统的设计，以更好地反映手语沟通的丰富性和复杂性。

Abstract: While sign language translation systems promise to enhance deaf people's access to information and communication, they have been met with strong skepticism from deaf communities due to risks of misrepresenting and oversimplifying the richness of signed communication in technologies. This article provides empirical evidence of the complexity of translation work involved in deaf communication through interviews with 13 deaf Chinese content creators who actively produce and share sign language content on video sharing platforms with both deaf and hearing audiences. By studying this unique group of content creators, our findings highlight the nuances of sign language translation, showing how deaf creators create content with multilingualism and multiculturalism in mind, support meaning making across languages and cultures, and navigate politics involved in their translation work. Grounded in these deaf-led translation practices, we draw on the sociolinguistic concept of (trans)languaging to re-conceptualize and reimagine the design of sign language translation systems.

</details>


### [122] [Investigating the Effects of Eco-Friendly Service Options on Rebound Behavior in Ride-Hailing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10237)
*Albin Zeqiri,Michael Rietzler,Enrico Rukzio*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 环保服务选项（EFSOs）旨在减少个人碳排放，但其环保标签可能增加消费，削弱预期效果。研究通过网约车场景实验发现，缺乏明确环保反馈的EFSOs会增加网约车使用，便利性选择变得更可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环保服务选项（EFSOs）旨在减少个人碳排放，但存在反弹效应风险——环保标签可能使消费者增加消费，从而削弱其环保效果。HCI领域对此研究不足，特别是常见环保反馈方法如何影响这种反弹效应。

Method: 采用在线组内实验设计（N=75），在网约车场景中测试。参与者完成10次试验，涉及5种条件：无EFSO、EFSO-最小化、EFSO-CO2等价、EFSO-游戏化、EFSO-社交化。共50次选择，行程范围0.5-2.0英里（0.80-3.22公里），测量不同EFSO变体相对于无EFSO基线的网约车使用率变化。

Result: 缺乏明确环保反馈指标的EFSOs增加了网约车使用率。定性响应表明，EFSOs使基于便利性的选择变得更可接受。不同EFSO变体对行为影响存在差异。

Conclusion: 环保服务选项设计需要考虑反弹效应。缺乏明确环保反馈的EFSOs可能适得其反，增加消费。研究为设计考虑反弹效应的EFSOs提供了启示，强调需要更有效的环保反馈机制来避免意外后果。

Abstract: Eco-friendly service options (EFSOs) aim to reduce personal carbon emissions, yet their eco-friendly framing may permit increased consumption, weakening their intended impact. Such rebound effects remain underexamined in HCI, including how common eco-feedback approaches shape them. We investigate this in an online within-subjects experiment (N=75) in a ride-hailing context. Participants completed 10 trials for five conditions (No EFSO, EFSO - Minimal, EFSO - CO2 Equivalency, EFSO - Gamified, EFSO - Social), yielding 50 choices between walking and ride-hailing for trips ranging from 0.5mi - 2.0mi (0.80km - 3.22km). We measured how different EFSO variants affected ride-hailing uptake relative to a No EFSO baseline. EFSOs lacking explicit eco-feedback metrics increased ride-hailing uptake, and qualitative responses indicate that EFSOs can make convenience-driven choices more permissible. We conclude with implications for designing EFSOs that begin to take rebound effects into account.

</details>


### [123] [Actions Speak Louder Than Chats: Investigating AI Chatbot Age Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10251)
*Olivia Figueira,Pranathi Chamarthi,Tu Le,Athina Markopoulou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现流行AI聊天机器人能够通过对话估算用户年龄，但在识别出儿童用户后未采取任何保护措施，违反了自身政策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人被儿童广泛使用，但存在隐私和安全风险。虽然COPPA保护13岁以下儿童，聊天机器人平台也有禁止儿童使用的政策，但年龄验证机制的有效性尚未被研究。需要评估聊天机器人是否能够通过对话识别儿童用户并采取相应保护措施。

Method: 开发审计框架，通过编程方式与聊天机器人交互，使用包含1050个实验的综合年龄提示库（包括隐式和显式年龄披露），分析聊天机器人的响应和行动。

Result: 研究发现聊天机器人能够估算用户年龄，但在识别出儿童用户后未采取任何行动，这与它们自身的政策相矛盾。

Conclusion: 该研究的方法和发现为平台设计和监管提供了见解，并通过概念验证的聊天机器人年龄验证实现展示了保护儿童在线安全的可能性。

Abstract: AI chatbots are widely used by children and teens today, but they pose significant risks to youth's privacy and safety due to both increasingly personal conversations and potential exposure to unsafe content. While children under 13 are protected by the Children's Online Privacy Protection Act (COPPA), chatbot providers' own privacy policies may also provide protections, since they typically prohibit children from accessing their platforms. Age gating is often employed to restrict children online, but chatbot age gating in particular has not been studied. In this paper, we investigate whether popular consumer chatbots are (i) able to estimate users' ages based solely on their conversations, and (ii) whether they take action upon identifying children. To that end, we develop an auditing framework in which we programmatically interact with chatbots and conduct 1050 experiments using our comprehensive library of age-indicative prompts, including implicit and explicit age disclosures, to analyze the chatbots' responses and actions. We find that while chatbots are capable of estimating age, they do not take any action when children are identified, contradicting their own policies. Our methodology and findings provide insights for platform design, demonstrated by our proof-of-concept chatbot age gating implementation, and regulation to protect children online.

</details>


### [124] [ECHO: An Open Research Platform for Evaluation of Chat, Human Behavior, and Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10295)
*Jiqun Liu,Nischal Dinesh,Ran Yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ECHO是一个开源研究平台，支持对人与对话AI系统和Web搜索引擎交互进行可重复的混合方法研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低研究人机交互的技术门槛，支持跨学科研究人员进行可扩展、可重复的人类中心AI评估

Method: 提供统一低编码框架，集成同意和背景调查、聊天和搜索信息寻求会话、写作或判断任务、前后任务评估，记录细粒度交互轨迹

Result: 平台支持端到端实验工作流程，导出结构化数据集供下游分析，降低信息检索、HCI和社会科学研究人员的技术障碍

Conclusion: ECHO平台通过支持聊天和搜索以及灵活评估工具，赋能研究人员在不同信息获取范式下研究学习、决策和用户体验

Abstract: ECHO (Evaluation of Chat, Human behavior, and Outcomes) is an open research platform designed to support reproducible, mixed-method studies of human interaction with both conversational AI systems and Web search engines. It enables researchers from varying disciplines to orchestrate end-to-end experimental workflows that integrate consent and background surveys, chat-based and search-based information-seeking sessions, writing or judgment tasks, and pre- and post-task evaluations within a unified, low-coding-load framework. ECHO logs fine-grained interaction traces and participant responses, and exports structured datasets for downstream analysis. By supporting both chat and search alongside flexible evaluation instruments, ECHO lowers technical barriers for studying learning, decision making, and user experience across different information access paradigms, empowering researchers from information retrieval, HCI, and the social sciences to conduct scalable and reproducible human-centered AI evaluations.

</details>


### [125] [Disability-First AI Dataset Annotation: Co-designing Stuttered Speech Annotation Guidelines with People Who Stutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10403)
*Xinru Tang,Jingjin Li,Shaomei Wu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文通过口吃者语音数据标注的案例研究，探讨了由非专业人士标注残障相关数据集的问题，提出了整合残障者生活经验的标注实践，强调具身知识对提升数据集质量的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI数据集中残障人群的代表性有所增加，但可访问性数据集通常由缺乏残障专业知识的人群工作者标注，导致标签不一致或不准确。特别是口吃者语音数据的标注面临挑战，因为口吃具有变异性且对其表现形式的看法存在分歧。

Method: 通过访谈和协同设计工作坊，与口吃者和领域专家合作，识别口吃语音标注的挑战，并开发整合口吃者生活经验的标注实践。

Result: 研究发现具身知识对提升数据集质量具有重要价值，同时揭示了残障经验的复杂性与静态标签的刚性之间的张力。开发了整合残障者生活经验的标注方法。

Conclusion: 论文提出了对AI流程中数据解释的残障优先和多重性感知方法的影响，强调需要更灵活、包容的标注框架来准确反映残障经验的多样性。

Abstract: Despite efforts to increase the representation of disabled people in AI datasets, accessibility datasets are often annotated by crowdworkers without disability-specific expertise, leading to inconsistent or inaccurate labels. This paper examines these annotation challenges through a case study of annotating speech data from people who stutter (PWS). Given the variability of stuttering and differing views on how it manifests, annotating and transcribing stuttered speech remains difficult, even for trained professionals. Through interviews and co-design workshops with PWS and domain experts, we identify challenges in stuttered speech annotation and develop practices that integrate the lived experiences of PWS into the annotation process. Our findings highlight the value of embodied knowledge in improving dataset quality, while revealing tensions between the complexity of disability experiences and the rigidity of static labels. We conclude with implications for disability-first and multiplicity-aware approaches to data interpretation across the AI pipeline.

</details>


### [126] [Towards Affordable, Non-Invasive Real-Time Hypoglycemia Detection Using Wearable Sensor Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10407)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Vikas Ashok,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于多模态生理信号的非侵入性低血糖检测框架，结合GSR和HR信号，通过深度学习模型实现高灵敏度检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在CGM昂贵或不可及地区进行非侵入性低血糖检测的挑战，为资源有限医疗环境提供可负担的血糖监测方案

Method: 使用OhioT1DM 2018数据集，评估GSR、HR及其融合三种模态；构建端到端流程，包括预处理、时间窗口、特征提取（手工和序列）、早期/晚期融合策略；应用多种ML和深度学习模型（CNN、LSTM、GRU、TCN）

Result: 生理信号在低血糖前呈现明显自主神经模式；GSR与HR融合比单信号模型显著提升检测灵敏度和稳定性；多模态深度学习架构在召回率（临床最紧迫指标）上表现最可靠

Conclusion: 仅使用廉价非侵入性可穿戴传感器即可实现实时低血糖检测，为服务不足社区和低资源医疗环境提供了可行的血糖监测途径

Abstract: Accurately detecting hypoglycemia without invasive glucose sensors remains a critical challenge in diabetes management, particularly in regions where continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) is prohibitively expensive or clinically inaccessible. This extended study introduces a comprehensive, multimodal physiological framework for non-invasive hypoglycemia detection using wearable sensor signals. Unlike prior work limited to single-signal analysis, this chapter evaluates three physiological modalities, galvanic skin response (GSR), heart rate (HR), and their combined fusion, using the OhioT1DM 2018 dataset. We develop an end-to-end pipeline that integrates advanced preprocessing, temporal windowing, handcrafted and sequence-based feature extraction, early and late fusion strategies, and a broad spectrum of machine learning and deep temporal models, including CNNs, LSTMs, GRUs, and TCNs. Our results demonstrate that physiological signals exhibit distinct autonomic patterns preceding hypoglycemia and that combining GSR with HR consistently enhances detection sensitivity and stability compared to single-signal models. Multimodal deep learning architectures achieve the most reliable performance, particularly in recall, the most clinically urgent metric. Ablation studies further highlight the complementary contributions of each modality, strengthening the case for affordable, sensor-based glycemic monitoring. The findings show that real-time hypoglycemia detection is achievable using only inexpensive, non-invasive wearable sensors, offering a pathway toward accessible glucose monitoring in underserved communities and low-resource healthcare environments.

</details>


### [127] [Exploring the Feasibility of Full-Body Muscle Activation Sensing with Insole Pressure Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10442)
*Hao Zhou,Mahanth Gowda*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Press2Muscle：首个利用鞋垫压力传感器无创推断肌肉激活的系统，通过分析足部压力变化来监测全身肌肉活动，适用于移动健康和康复领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉激活监测对损伤预防和康复至关重要，但现有方法（如表面肌电图）依赖专业电极，不适合长期移动使用。移动健康市场缺乏实用的肌肉激活传感方案。

Method: 提出Press2Muscle系统，利用鞋垫压力传感器分析足部压力变化来推断肌肉激活。采用数据驱动方法动态调整对不同足部区域的依赖，并结合用户生物特征数据以提高对未见用户的泛化能力。

Result: 在30名用户的留一用户交叉验证中，Press2Muscle的均方根误差为0.025，比基于视频的方法提升19%。系统在用户人口统计、鞋类和行走表面等方面表现出良好的鲁棒性，并成功展示了肌肉不平衡检测和自由生活场景下的肌肉激活估计。

Conclusion: Press2Muscle证明了使用鞋垫压力传感器在真实世界环境中进行肌肉激活监测的可行性，为移动健康应用提供了实用、无创的肌肉活动监测方案。

Abstract: Muscle activation initiates contractions that drive human movement, and understanding it provides valuable insights for injury prevention and rehabilitation. Yet, sensing muscle activation is barely explored in the rapidly growing mobile health market. Traditional methods for muscle activation sensing rely on specialized electrodes, such as surface electromyography, making them impractical, especially for long-term usage. In this paper, we introduce Press2Muscle, the first system to unobtrusively infer muscle activation using insole pressure sensors. The key idea is to analyze foot pressure changes resulting from full-body muscle activation that drives movements. To handle variations in pressure signals due to differences in users' gait, weight, and movement styles, we propose a data-driven approach to dynamically adjust reliance on different foot regions and incorporate easily accessible biographical data to enhance Press2Muscle's generalization to unseen users. We conducted an extensive study with 30 users. Under a leave-one-user-out setting, Press2Muscle achieves a root mean square error of 0.025, marking a 19% improvement over a video-based counterpart. A robustness study validates Press2Muscle's ability to generalize across user demographics, footwear types, and walking surfaces. Additionally, we showcase muscle imbalance detection and muscle activation estimation under free-living settings with Press2Muscle, confirming the feasibility of muscle activation sensing using insole pressure sensors in real-world settings.

</details>


### [128] [Why Human Guidance Matters in Collaborative Vibe Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10473)
*Haoyu Hu,Raja Marjieh,Katherine M Collins,Chenyi Li,Thomas L. Griffiths,Ilia Sucholutsky,Nori Jacoby*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过实验框架比较人类主导、AI主导和混合协作的"氛围编程"模式，发现人类提供高层指令效果最佳，AI提供指令会导致性能崩溃，混合系统在人类保持方向控制而AI负责评估时表现最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI通过自然语言指令实现"氛围编程"，使专家和非专家都能无需实际编写代码而生成代码。然而，氛围编程对生产力和协作的累积影响，以及人类在这一过程中的角色仍不清楚。

Method: 引入一个受控实验框架来研究协作氛围编程，比较人类主导、AI主导和混合组。在16个实验中涉及604名人类参与者，分析不同协作模式下指令提供和评估的效果。

Result: 人类在迭代过程中提供的高层指令对氛围编程效果独特且有效，而AI提供的指令常常导致性能崩溃。混合系统在人类保持方向控制（提供指令）而将评估委托给AI时表现最佳。

Conclusion: 氛围编程的最佳协作模式是人类保持高层方向控制，AI负责评估任务，这种混合系统能够最大化生产力和协作效果，避免AI主导指令导致的性能问题。

Abstract: Writing code has been one of the most transformative ways for human societies to translate abstract ideas into tangible technologies. Modern AI is transforming this process by enabling experts and non-experts alike to generate code without actually writing code, but instead, through natural language instructions, or "vibe coding". While increasingly popular, the cumulative impact of vibe coding on productivity and collaboration, as well as the role of humans in this process, remains unclear. Here, we introduce a controlled experimental framework for studying collaborative vibe coding and use it to compare human-led, AI-led, and hybrid groups. Across 16 experiments involving 604 human participants, we show that people provide uniquely effective high-level instructions for vibe coding across iterations, whereas AI-provided instructions often result in performance collapse. We further demonstrate that hybrid systems perform best when humans retain directional control (providing the instructions), while evaluation is delegated to AI.

</details>


### [129] [Division of Labor and Collaboration Between Parents in Family Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10501)
*Ziyi Wang,Congrong Zhang,Jingying Deng,Xiaofan Hu,Jie Cai,Nan Gao,Chun Yu,Haining Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过访谈18位1-3年级学生家长，分析家庭作业辅导中的劳动分工，提出以关系维护为核心的AI设计框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭作业辅导是家庭生活中的高要求且易冲突的实践，家长缺乏针对性的支持来管理其认知和情感负担。现有研究对家庭作业辅导中的劳动分工及其复杂性关注不足

Method: 对18位1-3年级学生家长进行访谈，分析家庭作业相关劳动的分工与协调方式，探讨AI可能的干预点

Result: 发现三个关键见解：1) 家庭作业劳动包含物理、认知和情感三个维度，后两者常被忽视；2) 存在父-母-子三元动态的劳动分工，孩子的反馈是调整父母劳动的主要因素；3) 提出优先关系维护而非任务自动化或广泛劳动缓解的AI设计框架

Conclusion: 通过将劳动作为整合照护工作的视角，探索家庭情境中劳动的复杂性，为女权主义和照护导向的HCI研究做出贡献，并促进情境敏感的协同育儿实践发展

Abstract: Homework tutoring work is a demanding and often conflict-prone practice in family life, and parents often lack targeted support for managing its cognitive and emotional burdens. Through interviews with 18 parents of children in grades 1-3, we examine how homework-related labor is divided and coordinated between parents, and where AI might meaningfully intervene. We found three key insights: (1) Homework labor encompasses distinct dimensions: physical, cognitive, and emotional, with the latter two often remaining invisible. (2) We identified father-mother-child triadic dynamics in labor division, with children's feedback as the primary factor shaping parental labor adjustments. (3) Building on prior HCI research, we propose an AI design that prioritizes relationship maintenance over task automation or broad labor mitigation. By employing labor as a lens that integrates care work, we explore the complexities of labor within family contexts, contributing to feminist and care-oriented HCI and to the development of context-sensitive coparenting practices.

</details>


### [130] [Exploring the Interplay Between Voice, Personality, and Gender in Human-Agent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10535)
*Kai Alexander Hackney,Lucas Guarenti Zangari,Jhonathan Sora-Cardenas,Emmanuel Munoz,Sterling R. Kalogeras,Betsy DiSalvo,Pedro Guillermo Feijoo-Garcia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索用户-智能体同步性在人际感知中的作用，通过388名参与者评估四种人工语音的人格特质识别能力，发现女性智能体的人格特质能被显著区分，而男性智能体则不能，并观察到人格同步性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了促进有效的人机交互，需要识别影响智能体感知和接受度的特征，以及这些特征如何影响关系建立。本研究旨在探索用户-智能体同步性的作用，特别是人格和性别因素在智能体感知中的影响。

Method: 研究评估了388名参与者，让他们从四种人工语音中感知人格特质。这些语音基于人类样本选择和改编，采用2×2设计：性别（男性/女性）×人格（内向/外向）作为分组因素。通过参与者对智能体的人格感知评估来探索同步性效应。

Result: 参与者能够显著区分女性智能体的人格特质（内向/外向），但男性智能体的人格特质未能被一致区分。研究还发现了人格同步性证据：参与者倾向于认为第一个智能体与自己的人格更相似，这种效应主要由男性参与者驱动，特别是对男性智能体。

Conclusion: 本研究为人机交互设计提供了重要见解，表明用户-智能体人格和性别同步性在智能体感知中具有复杂交互作用。设计时应考虑性别和人格特征的匹配，特别是男性参与者对男性智能体的同步性效应，这对建立有效的人机关系具有重要意义。

Abstract: To foster effective human-agent interactions, designers need to identify characteristics that could affect how agents are perceived and accepted, and to what extent they could impact rapport-building. Aiming to explore the role of user-agent synchrony, we assessed 388 participants to determine whether they could perceive personality traits from four artificial voices we selected and adapted from human samples, considering gender (male or female) and personality (introvert or extrovert) as grouping factors. Our findings suggest that participants were able to significantly differentiate female agents by personality, while male agents were not consistently distinguished. We also observed evidence of personality synchrony, where participants tended to perceive the first agent as more similar to their own personality, with this effect driven mainly by male participants, especially toward male agents. This paper contributes findings and insights to consider the interplay of user-agent personality and gender synchrony in the design of human-agent interactions.

</details>


### [131] [Labor, Capital, and Machine: Toward a Labor Process Theory for HCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10548)
*Yigang Qin,EunJeong Cheon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍劳动过程理论（LPT）作为HCI领域分析资本主义下工作和计算系统设计的重要理论资源，提出了七个研究方向以深化技术中介工作体制的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI社区呼吁重新关注劳动问题和计算的政治经济学，但需要更多与劳动理论结合的工作来理解现代工作和工人。现有研究缺乏对劳动过程理论的系统运用，需要引入这一理论资源来加强结构分析能力。

Method: 1. 追溯劳动过程理论的发展历程（从马克思、布雷弗曼到布洛维等）；2. 系统考察HCI中关于劳动的文献，分析其主题焦点和概念、实证、设计方法；3. 基于LPT提出七个具体研究方向。

Result: 提出了七个基于LPT的HCI研究方向：区分劳动与工作、将工作实践与价值生产联系起来、向上研究管理层、分析同意与合法性、超越生产点、设计替代性制度、去自然化资产阶级设计。这些方向能够深化对技术中介工作体制的分析，为批判性和规范性设计提供信息，并加强与更广泛政治经济批判的联系。

Conclusion: 劳动过程理论是HCI领域进行结构分析和批判性设计的重要理论资源。通过应用LPT提出的七个研究方向，HCI研究能够更深入地分析技术中介的工作体制，为设计更具批判性和规范性的计算系统提供理论基础，并加强与政治经济批判的联系。

Abstract: The HCI community has called for renewed attention to labor issues and the political economy of computing. Yet much work remains in engaging with labor theory to better understand modern work and workers. This article traces the development of Labor Process Theory (LPT) -- from Karl Marx and Harry Braverman to Michael Burawoy and beyond -- and introduces it as an essential yet underutilized resource for structural analysis of work under capitalism and the design of computing systems. We examine HCI literature on labor, investigating focal themes and conceptual, empirical, and design approaches. Drawing from LPT, we offer directions for HCI research and practice: distinguish labor from work, link work practice to value production, study up the management, analyze consent and legitimacy, move beyond the point of production, design alternative institutions, and unnaturalize bourgeois designs. These directions can deepen analyses of tech-mediated workplace regimes, inform critical and normative designs, and strengthen the field's connection to broader political economic critique.

</details>


### [132] [From Interaction to Demonstration Quality in Virtual Reality: Effects of Interaction Modality and Visual Representation on Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10618)
*Robin Beierling,Manuel Scheibl,Jonas Dech,Abhijit Vyas,Anna-Lisa Vollmer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了三种VR输入配置（动作捕捉手套、带手部可视化的控制器、带控制器可视化的控制器）对用户体验和任务执行的影响，发现不同配置在效率和自然性之间存在权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR在训练和演示中的应用日益广泛，但输入设备及其可视化方式可能影响用户工作负荷和性能，导致次优演示或训练效果降低。需要确定哪种配置最适合特定任务类型

Method: 参与者使用三种VR输入配置（动作捕捉手套、带手部可视化的控制器、带控制器可视化的控制器）在模拟环境中执行厨房相关的日常生活活动（ADLs），包括物体放置、切割、清洁和倾倒。通过系统可用性量表和NASA任务负荷指数评估用户体验（RQ1），通过轨迹分割分析运动效率、不必要动作和执行精度来评估任务特定交互行为（RQ2）

Result: 整体可用性和工作负荷无显著差异，但轨迹分析显示不同配置有特定的执行行为：控制器在拾取放置类任务（如摆桌）中显著更快完成且运动变异性更小；动作捕捉手套产生更自然的运动且不必要动作更少，但在切割面包等注重方式的任务中运动模式变异性更大

Conclusion: 研究揭示了VR输入配置在效率和自然性之间的权衡，对优化VR训练、提高用户生成演示质量以及根据特定应用目标定制交互设计具有重要意义

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) is increasingly used for training and demonstration purposes including a variety of applications ranging from robot learning to rehabilitation. However, the choice of input device and its visualization might influence workload and thus user performance leading to suboptimal demonstrations or reduced training effects. This study investigates how different VR input configurations - motion capture gloves, controllers with hand visualization, and controllers with controller visualization - affect user experience and task execution, with the goal of identifying which configuration is best suited for which type of task. Participants performed various kitchen-related activities of daily living (ADLs), including object placement, cutting, cleaning, and pouring in a simulated environment. To address two research questions, we evaluated user experience using the System Usability Scale and NASA Task Load Index (RQ1), and task-specific interaction behavior (RQ2). The latter was assessed using trajectory segmentation, analyzing movement efficiency, unnecessary actions, and execution precision. While no significant differences in overall usability and workload were found, trajectory analysis revealed configuration-specific execution behaviors with different movement strategies. Controllers enabled significantly faster task completion with less movement variability in pick-and-place style tasks such as table setting. In contrast, motion capture gloves produced more natural movements with fewer unnecessary actions, but also showed greater variance in movement patterns for manner-oriented tasks such as cutting bread. These findings highlight trade-offs between efficiency and naturalism, and have implications for optimizing VR-based training, improving the quality of user-generated demonstrations, and tailoring interaction design to specific application goals.

</details>


### [133] [Privacy Control in Conversational LLM Platforms: A Walkthrough Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10684)
*Zhuoyang Li,Yanlai Wu,Yao Li,Xinning Gui,Yuhan Luo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究分析了六个对话式LLM平台的数据控制机制，揭示了对话LLM平台中数据控制的新范式，并为平台开发者、政策制定者和研究者提供了实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM通过对话界面日益融入日常生活，它们通过自然语言输入处理用户数据并展现高级推理能力，这引发了用户对隐私控制的新担忧。虽然大量研究关注隐私风险，但对这些平台提供的数据控制机制关注较少。

Method: 研究分析了六个对话式LLM平台，考察它们如何定义和实施用户访问、编辑、删除和共享数据的功能。

Result: 分析揭示了对话LLM平台中数据控制的新范式：用户数据通过交互本身生成和衍生；自然语言提供灵活但往往模糊的控制；多用户与共享数据的交互引发了共同所有权和治理问题。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现，研究为平台开发者、政策制定者和研究者提供了实用见解，以设计更有效和可用的LLM驱动对话交互中的隐私控制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into daily life through conversational interfaces, processing user data via natural language inputs and exhibiting advanced reasoning capabilities, which raises new concerns about user control over privacy. While much research has focused on potential privacy risks, less attention has been paid to the data control mechanisms these platforms provide. This study examines six conversational LLM platforms, analyzing how they define and implement features for users to access, edit, delete, and share data. Our analysis reveals an emerging paradigm of data control in conversational LLM platforms, where user data is generated and derived through interaction itself, natural language enables flexible yet often ambiguous control, and multi-user interactions with shared data raise questions of co-ownership and governance. Based on these findings, we offer practical insights for platform developers, policymakers, and researchers to design more effective and usable privacy controls in LLM-powered conversational interactions.

</details>


### [134] [Don't blame me: How Intelligent Support Affects Moral Responsibility in Human Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10701)
*Cedric Faas,Richard Uth,Sarah Sterz,Markus Langer,Anna Maria Feit*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨了决策支持系统限制可用干预选项如何影响监督者的道德责任感知，特别是在系统出错时。实验发现当参与者被限制只能选择单一行动时，他们感到的道德责任更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键任务中，人类监督AI系统以降低风险并确保责任归属。然而，当决策支持系统提供错误建议时，可能会削弱人类监督的根本目的——真正的道德责任。本研究旨在探究限制干预选项的决策支持系统如何影响监督者的道德责任感知。

Method: 采用模拟监督实验，274名参与者监控面临十个关键情境的自主无人机，从六个可能行动中选择解决方案。AI系统将参与者的选择限制为六、四、二或仅一个选项（组间设计）。测量参与者在发生事故时的道德责任感知。

Result: 被限制只能选择单一行动的参与者感到的道德责任更少。同时，参与者对其他利益相关者（AI、AI开发者）的责任判断在不同条件下没有变化。

Conclusion: 研究结果为用户界面设计和监督架构提供了重要启示：应防止用户将道德能动性归因于AI，帮助他们理解道德责任如何分配，并在监督旨在防止伦理不良结果时，支持道德责任所需的认知和因果条件。

Abstract: AI-based systems can increasingly perform work tasks autonomously. In safety-critical tasks, human oversight of these systems is required to mitigate risks and to ensure responsibility in case something goes wrong. Since people often struggle to stay focused and perform good oversight, intelligent support systems are used to assist them, giving decision recommendations, alerting users, or restricting them from dangerous actions. However, in cases where recommendations are wrong, decision support might undermine the very reason why human oversight was employed -- genuine moral responsibility. The goal of our study was to investigate how a decision support system that restricted available interventions would affect overseer's perceived moral responsibility, in particular in cases where the support errs. In a simulated oversight experiment, participants (\textit{N}=274) monitored an autonomous drone that faced ten critical situations, choosing from six possible actions to resolve each situation. An AI system constrained participants' choices to either six, four, two, or only one option (between-subject study). Results showed that participants, who were restricted to choosing from a single action, felt less morally responsible if a crash occurred. At the same time, participants' judgments about the responsibility of other stakeholders (the AI; the developer of the AI) did not change between conditions. Our findings provide important insights for user interface design and oversight architectures: they should prevent users from attributing moral agency to AI, help them understand how moral responsibility is distributed, and, when oversight aims to prevent ethically undesirable outcomes, be designed to support the epistemic and causal conditions required for moral responsibility.

</details>


### [135] [The Effect of Design Thinking on Creative & Innovation Processes: An Empirical Study Across Different Design Experience Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10827)
*Yuxin Zhang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究使用线性回归和结构方程模型分析设计思维技能、创造性自我效能和集体创造性效能如何驱动设计创造力与创新，并检验模型在不同经验水平下的结构稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在阐明设计创造力与创新的多维驱动路径，为优化差异化的教学模型和专业实践指南提供实证基础。

Method: 采用线性回归和结构方程模型进行路径分析和中介效应检验，通过多组比较分析学生与专业群体间的模型差异。

Result: 四种设计思维技能均显著正向影响设计思维，设计思维显著正向预测设计创造力与创新。确认了三条显著的中介路径：CSE中介路径、CCE中介路径和"CSE→CCE"链式中介路径。学生与专业群体在全等价模型下存在显著差异，但在放松特定约束后嵌套模型无显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了设计创造力与创新的多维驱动机制，为针对不同经验水平的差异化教学和实践提供了实证依据。

Abstract: This study employs linear regression and structural equation modeling to explore how Thinking Skills, Design Thinking, Creative Self-Efficacy (CSE), and Collective Creative Efficacy (CCE) drive Design Creativity & Innovation, and analyzes the structural stability of the model across different levels of experience. Path analysis results indicate that the four Design Thinking Skills, Problem-driven Design (beta = 0.198, p < 0.01), Information-driven Design (beta = 0.241, p < 0.001), Solution-driven Design (beta = 0.227, p < 0.001), and Knowledge-driven Design (beta = 0.263, p < 0.001) all significantly and positively influence Design Thinking. Furthermore, Design Thinking has a significant positive predictive effect on Design Creativity & Innovation (beta = 0.286, p < 0.001). Mediation analysis confirms three significant mediation paths: the CSE mediation path (beta = 0.128, p < 0.001), the CCE mediation path (beta = 0.073, p < 0.01), and the "CSE to CCE" chain mediation path (beta = 0.025, p < 0.01). Multi-group comparison results reveal significant differences between the student and professional groups under the full equivalence model. After relaxing specific constraints, there were no significant differences between the nested models of the baseline model, partial measurement invariance, structural weight invariance, and structural covariance invariance. These findings elucidate the multi-dimensional pathways of Design Creativity & Innovation, providing a robust empirical basis for optimizing differentiated pedagogical models and professional practice guidelines.

</details>


### [136] [Viewpoint Recommendation for Point Cloud Labeling through Interaction Cost Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10871)
*Yu Zhang,Xinyi Zhao,Chongke Bi,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于3D点云语义分割标注的视点推荐方法，通过建模套索选择时间成本来推荐最小化标注时间的视点，从而减少标注时间成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云语义分割需要大量标注数据，但点云标注耗时费力，特别是需要调整相机视点和使用套索工具选择点。现有标注过程效率低下，需要减少标注时间成本。

Method: 1. 将Fitts定律适配到点云套索选择时间成本建模；2. 基于建模的时间成本，推荐最小化套索选择时间的视点；3. 构建集成视点推荐方法的点云语义分割数据标注系统。

Result: 1. 消融研究表明该方法有效减少了数据标注时间成本；2. 在不同数据集上与先前视点选择方法进行了定性比较，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的视点推荐方法能够显著减少3D点云语义分割的标注时间，通过优化标注视点提高了标注效率，为点云数据标注提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of 3D point clouds is important for many applications, such as autonomous driving. To train semantic segmentation models, labeled point cloud segmentation datasets are essential. Meanwhile, point cloud labeling is time-consuming for annotators, which typically involves tuning the camera viewpoint and selecting points by lasso. To reduce the time cost of point cloud labeling, we propose a viewpoint recommendation approach to reduce annotators' labeling time costs. We adapt Fitts' law to model the time cost of lasso selection in point clouds. Using the modeled time cost, the viewpoint that minimizes the lasso selection time cost is recommended to the annotator. We build a data labeling system for semantic segmentation of 3D point clouds that integrates our viewpoint recommendation approach. The system enables users to navigate to recommended viewpoints for efficient annotation. Through an ablation study, we observed that our approach effectively reduced the data labeling time cost. We also qualitatively compare our approach with previous viewpoint selection approaches on different datasets.

</details>


### [137] [What do people want to fact-check?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10935)
*Bijean Ghafouri,Dorsaf Sallami,Luca Luceri,Taylor Lynn Curtis,Jean-Francois Godbout,Emilio Ferrara,Reihaneh Rabbany*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究首次大规模分析公众实际向AI事实核查系统提交的查询内容，发现用户查询范围广泛但认知模式单一，约1/4请求无法实证解决，且与标准评估数据集存在显著结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息研究主要关注供给方（如虚假信息内容、生产者、纠正效果），而忽视了需求方的基本问题：当普通人可以核查任何信息时，他们实际会询问什么？

Method: 分析457名参与者向开放式AI事实核查系统提交的近2,500条陈述，每条陈述按五个语义维度（领域、认知形式、可验证性、目标实体、时间参照）进行分类，构建公众核查需求的行为地图。

Result: 1. 用户话题范围广泛但认知模式单一，主要提交关于当前可观察事物的简单描述性陈述；2. 约1/4请求涉及无法实证解决的陈述（道德判断、推测性预测、主观评价），显示用户需求与工具能力不匹配；3. 与FEVER基准数据集比较显示五个维度均存在显著结构差异，表明标准评估语料库编码的是合成声明环境，与真实世界核查需求不符。

Conclusion: 研究将事实核查重新定义为需求驱动问题，指出当前AI系统和基准测试与人们实际经历的不确定性存在错位，揭示了用户需求与事实核查工具能力之间的系统性不匹配。

Abstract: Research on misinformation has focused almost exclusively on supply, asking what falsehoods circulate, who produces them, and whether corrections work. A basic demand-side question remains unanswered. When ordinary people can fact-check anything they want, what do they actually ask about? We provide the first large-scale evidence on this question by analyzing close to 2{,}500 statements submitted by 457 participants to an open-ended AI fact-checking system. Each claim is classified along five semantic dimensions (domain, epistemic form, verifiability, target entity, and temporal reference), producing a behavioral map of public verification demand. Three findings stand out. First, users range widely across topics but default to a narrow epistemic repertoire, overwhelmingly submitting simple descriptive claims about present-day observables. Second, roughly one in four requests concerns statements that cannot be empirically resolved, including moral judgments, speculative predictions, and subjective evaluations, revealing a systematic mismatch between what users seek from fact-checking tools and what such tools can deliver. Third, comparison with the FEVER benchmark dataset exposes sharp structural divergences across all five dimensions, indicating that standard evaluation corpora encode a synthetic claim environment that does not resemble real-world verification needs. These results reframe fact-checking as a demand-driven problem and identify where current AI systems and benchmarks are misaligned with the uncertainty people actually experience.

</details>


### [138] [Reality Copilot: Voice-First Human-AI Collaboration in Mixed Reality Using Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11025)
*Liuchuan Yu,Yongqi Zhang,Lap-Fai Yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Reality Copilot：基于大型多模态模型的混合现实语音助手，支持环境理解、3D内容生成和实时信息检索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型多模态模型主要局限于传统界面（桌面/手机），而混合现实系统缺乏与大规模AI模型的集成，导致智能辅助能力有限。需要探索LMM在混合现实中的人机协作设计空间。

Method: 开发Reality Copilot系统，采用语音优先的交互方式，利用LMM实现自然语音交互。系统支持物理环境上下文理解、逼真3D内容生成、实时信息检索，并支持跨平台工作流。

Result: 提出了一个完整的混合现实AI助手系统，能够通过语音指令理解环境、生成3D内容、获取信息，并支持生成上下文感知的文本内容和导出生成资产。

Conclusion: Reality Copilot展示了LMM在混合现实中实现自然语音交互和人机协作的潜力，为LMM驱动的混合现实应用开辟了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have shown strong potential for assisting users in tasks, such as programming, content creation, and information access, yet their interaction remains largely limited to traditional interfaces such as desktops and smartphones. Meanwhile, advances in mixed reality (MR) hardware have enabled applications that extend beyond entertainment and into everyday use. However, most existing MR systems rely primarily on manual input (e.g., hand gestures or controllers) and provide limited intelligent assistance due to the lack of integration with large-scale AI models. We present Reality Copilot, a voice-first human-AI assistant for mixed reality that leverages LMMs to enable natural speech-based interaction. The system supports contextual understanding of physical environments, realistic 3D content generation, and real-time information retrieval. In addition to in-headset interaction, Reality Copilot facilitates cross-platform workflows by generating context-aware textual content and exporting generated assets. This work explores the design space of LMM-powered human-AI collaboration in mixed reality.

</details>


### [139] [Normalized Surveillance in the Datafied Car: How Autonomous Vehicle Users Rationalize Privacy Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11026)
*Yehuda Perry,Tawfiq Ammari*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过访谈发现自动驾驶汽车用户对车辆监控表现出较少隐私担忧，将其视为平台监控生态的自然延伸，揭示了数据化汽车作为"泄漏家庭"移动扩展的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车通过传感器实现广泛数据化和监控，但用户如何理解这种车辆监控在日常数据化环境中的意义尚不清楚。研究旨在探讨用户在平台监控生态中对自动驾驶汽车监控的认知和接受程度。

Method: 采用建构主义扎根理论方法，对16名自动驾驶汽车驾驶员进行半结构化访谈，分析用户对车辆监控的理解和态度。

Result: 研究发现驾驶员对自动驾驶汽车特有的隐私担忧较少，通过将其与现有数字平台监控相比较而将监控正常化。数据化汽车被视为"泄漏家庭"的移动延伸，用户将自动驾驶汽车数据化视为平台数据提取生态中的又一节点。地理数据访问限制（如仅限加州访问驾驶日志）造成不对称性，阻碍知情隐私审议，形成"三级数字鸿沟"。

Conclusion: 研究提出治理干预措施以促进社会学习民主化，包括通用数据访问权、具有约束力的透明度要求、数据最小化标准，以防止汽车数据化领域的"竞次"动态。机器学习对数据密集型方法的依赖创造了超越个体制造商选择的监控结构压力。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) are characterized by pervasive datafication and surveillance through sensors like in-cabin cameras, LIDAR, and GPS. Drawing on 16 semi-structured interviews with AV drivers analyzed using constructivist grounded theory, this study examines how users make sense of vehicular surveillance within everyday datafication. Findings reveal drivers demonstrate few AV-specific privacy concerns, instead normalizing monitoring through comparisons with established digital platforms. We theorize this indifference by situating AV surveillance within the `surveillance ecology' of platform environments, arguing the datafied car functions as a mobile extension of the `leaky home' -- private spaces rendered permeable through connected technologies continuously transmitting behavioral data.
  The study contributes to scholarship on surveillance beliefs, datafication, and platform governance by demonstrating how users who have accepted comprehensive smartphone and smart home monitoring encounter AV datafication as just another node in normalized data extraction. We highlight how geographic restrictions on data access -- currently limiting driver log access to California -- create asymmetries that impede informed privacy deliberation, exemplifying `tertiary digital divides.' Finally, we examine how machine learning's reliance on data-intensive approaches creates structural pressure for surveillance that transcends individual manufacturer choices. We propose governance interventions to democratize social learning, including universal data access rights, binding transparency requirements, and data minimization standards to prevent race-to-the-bottom dynamics in automotive datafication.

</details>


### [140] [AI Sensing and Intervention in Higher Education: Student Perceptions of Learning Impacts, Affective Responses, and Ethical Priorities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11074)
*Bingyi Han,Ying Ma,Simon Coghlan,Dana McKay,George Buchanan,Wally Smith*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨学生对AI感知干预学习的态度，通过实验发现学生重视针对性干预但反感AI监控，偏好系统提示而非教师干预，最关注自主权和隐私等伦理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI技术通过感知学生注意力和情绪来实现个性化教学干预日益普及，但关于学生学习、福祉和伦理的问题亟待关注。特别是学生对于AI感知干预学习的观点常被忽视，需要了解学生对不同AI监控方式和干预形式的看法及其伦理考量。

Method: 采用在线混合方法实验，招募132名澳大利亚大学生，通过视频场景展示不同变量：是否使用感知（使用vs未使用）、感知模态（基于注视的注意力检测vs基于面部的情感检测）、干预方式（数字设备vs教师）。参与者还完成配对排序任务，对六个核心伦理问题进行优先级排序。

Result: 学生重视针对性干预但对AI监控持负面态度，不受感知方法影响；偏好系统生成的提示而非教师发起的帮助，主要出于学习自主性和社交尴尬的考虑；伦理考量优先级依次为：自主权和隐私、透明度、准确性、公平性、学习效益。

Conclusion: 应设计可定制、社交敏感、非侵入性的系统，保护学生的控制权、自主性和福祉，同时考虑学生对AI监控的负面态度和对学习自主性的重视。

Abstract: AI technologies that sense student attention and emotions to enable more personalised teaching interventions are increasingly promoted, but raise pressing questions about student learning, well-being, and ethics. In particular, students' perspectives about AI sensing-intervention in learning are often overlooked. We conducted an online mixed-method experiment with Australian university students (N=132), presenting video scenarios varying by whether sensing was used (in-use vs. not-in-use), sensing modality (gaze-based attention detection vs. facial-based emotion detection), and intervention (by digital device vs. teacher). Participants also completed pairwise ranking tasks to prioritise six core ethical concerns. Findings revealed that students valued targeted intervention but responded negatively to AI monitoring, regardless of sensing methods. Students preferred system-generated hints over teacher-initiated assistance, citing learning agency and social embarrassment concerns. Students' ethical considerations prioritised autonomy and privacy, followed by transparency, accuracy, fairness, and learning beneficence. We advocate designing customisable, social-sensitive, non-intrusive systems that preserve student control, agency, and well-being.

</details>


### [141] [LCIP: Loss-Controlled Inverse Projection of High-Dimensional Image Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11141)
*Yu Wang,Frederik L. Dennig,Michael Behrisch,Alexandru Telea*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种新的逆投影方法，能够通过用户控制参数"扫描"数据空间，克服现有方法只能生成固定表面结构的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆投影方法存在根本性限制——只能生成数据空间中的固定表面结构，无法覆盖数据空间的丰富性。需要一种能够灵活探索数据空间的方法

Method: 开发了一种通用的逆投影方法，适用于任何投影技术和数据集，通过两个直观的用户设定参数控制，能够"扫描"数据空间，实现简单易用

Result: 通过广泛的图像风格迁移应用展示了方法的有效性，证明了其能够灵活探索数据空间并支持多种任务

Conclusion: 提出的新方法克服了现有逆投影方法的局限性，能够通过用户控制参数灵活探索数据空间，为数据增强、分类器分析和数据插补等任务提供了更强大的工具

Abstract: Projections (or dimensionality reduction) methods $P$ aim to map high-dimensional data to typically 2D scatterplots for visual exploration. Inverse projection methods $P^{-1}$ aim to map this 2D space to the data space to support tasks such as data augmentation, classifier analysis, and data imputation. Current $P^{-1}$ methods suffer from a fundamental limitation -- they can only generate a fixed surface-like structure in data space, which poorly covers the richness of this space. We address this by a new method that can `sweep' the data space under user control. Our method works generically for any $P$ technique and dataset, is controlled by two intuitive user-set parameters, and is simple to implement. We demonstrate it by an extensive application involving image manipulation for style transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Rethinking Security of Diffusion-based Generative Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10219)
*Jihao Zhu,Zixuan Chen,Jiali Liu,Lingxiao Yang,Yi Zhou,Weiqi Luo,Xiaohua Xie*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于扩散模型的生成式图像隐写术（DM-GIS）的安全性，发现噪声分布是关键因素，并提出了一种基于噪声空间的扩散隐写分析器（NS-DSer）来检测DM-GIS生成的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像隐写术（GIS）能够在生成图像时直接嵌入秘密信息，无需依赖现有载体图像。最近出现了多种基于扩散模型的DM-GIS方法，它们能有效对抗传统隐写分析技术。然而，这些方法的安全性尚未得到充分评估，需要深入分析影响DM-GIS安全性的关键因素。

Method: 首先分析现有DM-GIS方法的通用流程，发现扩散模型的噪声空间是主要的嵌入域。然后理论分析DM-GIS安全性与扩散模型噪声分布的关系，证明任何破坏噪声分布的隐写操作都会损害安全性。基于这一洞察，提出噪声空间扩散隐写分析器（NS-DSer），这是一个简单有效的隐写分析框架，可在扩散模型噪声空间中检测DM-GIS生成的图像。

Result: 实验验证了DM-GIS安全性的理论分析，并证明了NS-DSer在各种检测场景中的有效性。通过NS-DSer重新评估了现有DM-GIS方法的安全性，结果显示在日益复杂的检测场景下，这些方法存在可检测的安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的噪声分布是影响DM-GIS安全性的关键因素，任何破坏噪声分布的隐写操作都会降低安全性。提出的NS-DSer框架能够有效检测DM-GIS生成的图像，为评估和改进生成式隐写术的安全性提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Generative image steganography is a technique that conceals secret messages within generated images, without relying on pre-existing cover images. Recently, a number of diffusion model-based generative image steganography (DM-GIS) methods have been introduced, which effectively combat traditional steganalysis techniques. In this paper, we identify the key factors that influence DM-GIS security and revisit the security of existing methods. Specifically, we first provide an overview of the general pipelines of current DM-GIS methods, finding that the noise space of diffusion models serves as the primary embedding domain. Further, we analyze the relationship between DM-GIS security and noise distribution of diffusion models, theoretically demonstrating that any steganographic operation that disrupts the noise distribution compromise DM-GIS security. Building on this insight, we propose a Noise Space-based Diffusion Steganalyzer (NS-DSer)-a simple yet effective steganalysis framework allowing for detecting DM-GIS generated images in the diffusion model noise space. We reevaluate the security of existing DM-GIS methods using NS-DSer across increasingly challenging detection scenarios. Experimental results validate our theoretical analysis of DM-GIS security and show the effectiveness of NS-DSer across diverse detection scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Chamfer-Linkage for Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10444)
*Kishen N Gowda,Willem Fletcher,MohammadHossein Bateni,Laxman Dhulipala,D Ellis Hershkowitz,Rajesh Jayaram,Jakub Łącki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Chamfer-linkage作为层次凝聚聚类的新连接函数，使用Chamfer距离衡量簇间距离，相比传统连接函数在多样数据集上产生更高质量的聚类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统层次凝聚聚类（HAC）的连接函数（如单连接、平均连接、Ward方法）在实际应用中表现不稳定，质量参差不齐，缺乏一致的高质量聚类结果。HAC本身不优化单一显式全局目标，因此连接函数的选择对聚类质量至关重要。

Method: 提出Chamfer-linkage连接函数，使用Chamfer距离（机器学习与计算机视觉中常用的点云距离度量）来衡量簇间距离。该方法具有O(n²)时间复杂度，与传统连接函数效率相当，可作为即插即用的替代方案。

Result: 理论证明Chamfer-linkage HAC可实现O(n²)时间复杂度。实验表明，在多样化数据集上，Chamfer-linkage相比平均连接和Ward方法等传统连接函数，能一致地产生更高质量的聚类结果。

Conclusion: Chamfer-linkage是一种实用的层次聚类连接函数，满足概念表示的理想特性，可作为传统连接函数的有效替代，扩展了层次聚类在理论和实践中的工具集。

Abstract: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering (HAC) is a widely-used clustering method based on repeatedly merging the closest pair of clusters, where inter-cluster distances are determined by a linkage function. Unlike many clustering methods, HAC does not optimize a single explicit global objective; clustering quality is therefore primarily evaluated empirically, and the choice of linkage function plays a crucial role in practice. However, popular classical linkages, such as single-linkage, average-linkage and Ward's method show high variability across real-world datasets and do not consistently produce high-quality clusterings in practice.
  In this paper, we propose \emph{Chamfer-linkage}, a novel linkage function that measures the distance between clusters using the Chamfer distance, a popular notion of distance between point-clouds in machine learning and computer vision. We argue that Chamfer-linkage satisfies desirable concept representation properties that other popular measures struggle to satisfy. Theoretically, we show that Chamfer-linkage HAC can be implemented in $O(n^2)$ time, matching the efficiency of classical linkage functions. Experimentally, we find that Chamfer-linkage consistently yields higher-quality clusterings than classical linkages such as average-linkage and Ward's method across a diverse collection of datasets. Our results establish Chamfer-linkage as a practical drop-in replacement for classical linkage functions, broadening the toolkit for hierarchical clustering in both theory and practice.

</details>


### [144] [Signature-Kernel Based Evaluation Metrics for Robust Probabilistic and Tail-Event Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10182)
*Benjamin R. Redhead,Thomas L. Lee,Peng Gu,Víctor Elvira,Amos Storkey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种基于核的度量方法：Sig-MMD和CSig-MMD，用于解决概率预测评估中现有方法忽略时空依赖性和尾部事件敏感性的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前概率预测评估框架缺乏共识度量标准，存在两个关键缺陷：假设时间步或变量间独立，以及对尾部事件缺乏敏感性。尾部事件在实际决策中最为关键，但现有评估方法无法有效衡量预测模型对这些极端事件的预测能力。

Method: 提出两种基于核的度量方法：1) 签名最大均值差异(Sig-MMD)，利用签名核捕捉复杂的变量间和时间依赖关系，对缺失数据具有鲁棒性；2) 新颖的截断Sig-MMD(CSig-MMD)，引入截断方案，优先考虑预测尾部事件的能力，同时严格保持适当性这一良好评分规则的重要属性。

Result: 这些度量方法能够更可靠地评估直接多步预测，促进更稳健的概率算法开发。Sig-MMD和CSig-MMD能够捕捉复杂的时空依赖关系，CSig-MMD特别关注尾部事件预测能力，同时保持度量标准的适当性。

Conclusion: 提出的Sig-MMD和CSig-MMD度量解决了当前概率预测评估框架的关键缺陷，特别是对时空依赖性和尾部事件敏感性的不足。这些方法为概率预测提供了更全面、更可靠的评估工具，有助于开发在实际高风险领域中更有效的预测算法。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting is increasingly critical across high-stakes domains, from finance and epidemiology to climate science. However, current evaluation frameworks lack a consensus metric and suffer from two critical flaws: they often assume independence across time steps or variables, and they demonstrably lack sensitivity to tail events, the very occurrences that are most pivotal in real-world decision-making. To address these limitations, we propose two kernel-based metrics: the signature maximum mean discrepancy (Sig-MMD) and our novel censored Sig-MMD (CSig-MMD). By leveraging the signature kernel, these metrics capture complex inter-variate and inter-temporal dependencies and remain robust to missing data. Furthermore, CSig-MMD introduces a censoring scheme that prioritizes a forecaster's capability to predict tail events while strictly maintaining properness, a vital property for a good scoring rule. These metrics enable a more reliable evaluation of direct multi-step forecasting, facilitating the development of more robust probabilistic algorithms.

</details>


### [145] [Kernel-Based Learning of Chest X-ray Images for Predicting ICU Escalation among COVID-19 Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10261)
*Qiyuan Shi,Jian Kang,Yi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GLIMARK方法，将多核学习扩展到指数族分布结果变量，应用于COVID-19胸部X光数据预测ICU升级


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多核学习方法主要针对连续结果变量，无法处理更广泛的指数族分布数据（如二分类、计数等），限制了在现实世界异质数据分析中的应用

Method: 提出GLIMARK方法，将多核学习扩展到广义线性模型框架，通过组合多个简单核函数构建复合核，集成异质数据源信息，适用于指数族分布结果变量

Result: GLIMARK能有效恢复或近似真实数据生成机制，在COVID-19胸部X光数据集中成功预测ICU升级的二元结果，并提取出具有临床意义的特征

Conclusion: GLIMARK扩展了多核学习的适用范围，为处理指数族分布结果变量提供了有效方法，在医疗诊断等实际应用中具有实用价值

Abstract: Kernel methods have been extensively utilized in machine learning for classification and prediction tasks due to their ability to capture complex non-linear data patterns. However, single kernel approaches are inherently limited, as they rely on a single type of kernel function (e.g., Gaussian kernel), which may be insufficient to fully represent the heterogeneity or multifaceted nature of real-world data. Multiple kernel learning (MKL) addresses these limitations by constructing composite kernels from simpler ones and integrating information from heterogeneous sources. Despite these advances, traditional MKL methods are primarily designed for continuous outcomes. We extend MKL to accommodate the outcome variable belonging to the exponential family, representing a broader variety of data types, and refer to our proposed method as generalized linear models with integrated multiple additive regression with kernels (GLIMARK). Empirically, we demonstrate that GLIMARK can effectively recover or approximate the true data-generating mechanism. We have applied it to a COVID-19 chest X-ray dataset, predicting binary outcomes of ICU escalation and extracting clinically meaningful features, underscoring the practical utility of this approach in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [146] [Don't Eliminate Cut: Exponential Separations in LLM-Based Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10512)
*Sho Sonoda,Shunta Akiyama,Yuya Uezato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为LLM引导的形式化定理证明建立了理论分析框架，通过将策略提议建模为有限时域确定性MDP中的随机策略，解释了最坏情况困难与经验成功之间的差距，并证明了具有子目标分解的分层学习器相比平面学习器具有指数级数据效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为LLM引导的交互式定理证明（如Lean）建立理论分析框架，解释为什么在实际应用中LLM能够成功辅助定理证明，尽管从最坏情况复杂度角度看这是困难的问题。作者希望从理论层面理解现代表示学习在形式化定理证明中的作用，并为智能体式定理证明器中的子目标分解提供理论依据。

Method: 1. 将策略提议建模为有限时域确定性马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中的随机策略；2. 将状态和动作空间视为一般紧致度量空间，假设Lipschitz策略以捕捉现代表示学习；3. 引入由参考策略q生成的问题分布，包括包含可重用切割/引理/草图结构的潜在变量模型（用证明DAG表示）；4. 在top-k搜索协议和Tsybakov型边界条件下，推导有限时域成功概率的下界；5. 通过证明DAG深度D与大小关系分析学习复杂度。

Result: 1. 推导出有限时域成功概率的下界，可分解为搜索和学习两项，其中学习项由顺序Rademacher/覆盖复杂度控制；2. 主要分离结果表明：当切割消除将深度为D的DAG扩展为大小为Ω(Λ^D)的无切割树，而切割感知的分层过程大小为O(λ^D)且λ≪Λ时，平面（无切割）学习器相比切割感知的分层学习器需要指数级更多的数据；3. 为最近智能体式定理证明器中的子目标分解提供了原则性理论依据。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM引导的形式化定理证明建立了理论分析框架，解释了经验成功与最坏情况困难之间的差距。关键理论贡献是证明了在证明具有可重用结构（通过证明DAG表示）的情况下，能够利用切割/引理/草图的分层学习器相比忽略这些结构的平面学习器具有指数级的数据效率优势。这为智能体式定理证明器中子目标分解的设计提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a theoretical analysis of LLM-guided formal theorem proving in interactive proof assistants (e.g., Lean) by modeling tactic proposal as a stochastic policy in a finite-horizon deterministic MDP. To capture modern representation learning, we treat the state and action spaces as general compact metric spaces and assume Lipschitz policies. To explain the gap between worst-case hardness and empirical success, we introduce problem distributions generated by a reference policy $q$, including a latent-variable model in which proofs exhibit reusable cut/lemma/sketch structure represented by a proof DAG. Under a top-$k$ search protocol and Tsybakov-type margin conditions, we derive lower bounds on finite-horizon success probability that decompose into search and learning terms, with learning controlled by sequential Rademacher/covering complexity. Our main separation result shows that when cut elimination expands a DAG of depth $D$ into a cut-free tree of size $Ω(Λ^D)$ while the cut-aware hierarchical process has size $O(λ^D)$ with $λ\llΛ$, a flat (cut-free) learner provably requires exponentially more data than a cut-aware hierarchical learner. This provides a principled justification for subgoal decomposition in recent agentic theorem provers.

</details>


### [147] [Large Language Models Predict Functional Outcomes after Acute Ischemic Stroke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10119)
*Anjali K. Kapoor,Anton Alyakin,Jin Vivian Lee,Eunice Yang,Annelene M. Schulze,Krithik Vishwanath,Jinseok Lee,Yindalon Aphinyanaphongs,Howard Riina,Jennifer A. Frontera,Eric Karl Oermann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了大型语言模型直接从急性缺血性卒中入院记录预测功能结局（mRS评分）的能力，发现微调后的Llama模型在90天mRS预测上达到33.9%的准确率，与基于结构化数据的基线模型性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测急性缺血性卒中后的功能结局对临床决策和资源分配至关重要。以往研究主要依赖结构化变量（如年龄、NIHSS）和传统机器学习方法，而大型语言模型直接从常规入院记录推断未来mRS评分的能力尚未充分探索。

Method: 研究评估了编码器模型（BERT、NYUTron）和生成式模型（Llama-3.1-8B、MedGemma-4B）在冻结和微调两种设置下，使用纽约大学Langone卒中登记数据（2016-2025年）预测出院和90天mRS评分。数据集包括9,485份出院记录和1,898份90天随访记录，采用时间分割验证，最近12个月数据作为测试集。

Result: 微调后的Llama模型表现最佳：90天mRS预测的7分类准确率为33.9%（95% CI 27.9-39.9%），二分类（mRS 0-2 vs 3-6）准确率为76.3%（95% CI 70.7-81.9%）。出院预测的7分类准确率为42.0%（95% CI 39.0-45.0%），二分类准确率为75.0%（95% CI 72.4-77.6%）。在90天预测任务中，Llama模型与基于结构化数据（NIHSS和年龄）的基线模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 微调的大型语言模型能够仅从入院记录预测卒中后功能结局，其性能与需要结构化变量提取的模型相当。这一发现支持开发基于文本的预后工具，这些工具无需手动数据提取即可无缝集成到临床工作流程中。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of functional outcomes after acute ischemic stroke can inform clinical decision-making and resource allocation. Prior work on modified Rankin Scale (mRS) prediction has relied primarily on structured variables (e.g., age, NIHSS) and conventional machine learning. The ability of large language models (LLMs) to infer future mRS scores directly from routine admission notes remains largely unexplored. We evaluated encoder (BERT, NYUTron) and generative (Llama-3.1-8B, MedGemma-4B) LLMs, in both frozen and fine-tuned settings, for discharge and 90-day mRS prediction using a large, real-world stroke registry. The discharge outcome dataset included 9,485 History and Physical notes and the 90-day outcome dataset included 1,898 notes from the NYU Langone Get With The Guidelines-Stroke registry (2016-2025). Data were temporally split with the most recent 12 months held out for testing. Performance was assessed using exact (7-class) mRS accuracy and binary functional outcome (mRS 0-2 vs. 3-6) accuracy and compared against established structured-data baselines incorporating NIHSS and age. Fine-tuned Llama achieved the highest performance, with 90-day exact mRS accuracy of 33.9% [95% CI, 27.9-39.9%] and binary accuracy of 76.3% [95% CI, 70.7-81.9%]. Discharge performance reached 42.0% [95% CI, 39.0-45.0%] exact accuracy and 75.0% [95% CI, 72.4-77.6%] binary accuracy. For 90-day prediction, Llama performed comparably to structured-data baselines. Fine-tuned LLMs can predict post-stroke functional outcomes from admission notes alone, achieving performance comparable to models requiring structured variable abstraction. Our findings support the development of text-based prognostic tools that integrate seamlessly into clinical workflows without manual data extraction.

</details>


### [148] [$μ$pscaling small models: Principled warm starts and hyperparameter transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10545)
*Yuxin Ma,Nan Chen,Mateo Díaz,Soufiane Hayou,Dmitriy Kunisky,Soledad Villar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通用的模型放大方法，基于μP理论确保放大后模型与原始模型等效，并扩展了μTransfer技术用于放大模型超参数调优


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型放大方法对超参数敏感，需要在大模型上直接调优，成本过高。不清楚在小模型上调优后通过超参数缩放定律外推的方法在放大场景下是否仍然有效

Method: 1) 基于μP和任意维度架构理论，提出通用模型放大方法，确保放大模型与原始模型等效；2) 扩展μTransfer理论，开发用于放大模型的超参数转移技术

Result: 理论证明放大模型与加宽版本等效，允许无限宽度极限的严格分析。在真实数据集和架构上实证验证了超参数转移方法的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的方法为模型放大提供了理论基础和实用工具，解决了放大过程中超参数调优的效率和效果问题，使模型放大更加可靠和高效

Abstract: Modern large-scale neural networks are often trained and released in multiple sizes to accommodate diverse inference budgets. To improve efficiency, recent work has explored model upscaling: initializing larger models from trained smaller ones in order to transfer knowledge and accelerate convergence. However, this method can be sensitive to hyperparameters that need to be tuned at the target upscaled model size, which is prohibitively costly to do directly. It remains unclear whether the most common workaround -- tuning on smaller models and extrapolating via hyperparameter scaling laws -- is still sound when using upscaling. We address this with principled approaches to upscaling with respect to model widths and efficiently tuning hyperparameters in this setting. First, motivated by $μ$P and any-dimensional architectures, we introduce a general upscaling method applicable to a broad range of architectures and optimizers, backed by theory guaranteeing that models are equivalent to their widened versions and allowing for rigorous analysis of infinite-width limits. Second, we extend the theory of $μ$Transfer to a hyperparameter transfer technique for models upscaled using our method and empirically demonstrate that this method is effective on realistic datasets and architectures.

</details>


### [149] [TRACE: Theoretical Risk Attribution under Covariate-shift Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10588)
*Hosein Anjidani,S. Yahya S. R. Tehrani,Mohammad Mahdi Mojahedian,Mohammad Hossein Yassaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE框架将源域风险变化分解为四个可解释因素，提供诊断工具和部署门控评分，实现安全、标签高效的模型替换


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当源域训练的模型被基于偏移数据训练的新模型替换时，其在源域的性能可能发生不可预测的变化。需要理解这种风险变化的原因，以支持安全、标签高效的模型替换决策。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，将风险变化绝对值分解为四个可解释因素：两个泛化差距、模型变化惩罚和协变量偏移惩罚。协变量偏移惩罚通过模型敏感度因子（来自高分位数输入梯度）和数据偏移度量估计；默认使用特征空间最优传输，并提供基于最大均值差异的鲁棒替代方案。模型变化惩罚由两个模型在目标样本上的平均输出距离控制。泛化差距在保留数据上估计。

Result: 在线性回归理想化设置中验证TRACE边界正确捕捉真实风险差异随偏移幅度的缩放关系。在合成和视觉基准测试中，TRACE诊断有效且与真实性能退化保持强单调关系。推导出的部署门控评分与风险变化绝对值强相关，在门控决策中实现高AUROC/AUPRC。

Conclusion: TRACE框架为理解模型替换时的风险变化提供了可解释的分解方法，通过部署门控评分实现了安全、标签高效的模型替换决策支持。

Abstract: When a source-trained model $Q$ is replaced by a model $\tilde{Q}$ trained on shifted data, its performance on the source domain can change unpredictably. To address this, we study the two-model risk change, $ΔR := R_P(\tilde{Q}) - R_P(Q)$, under covariate shift. We introduce TRACE (Theoretical Risk Attribution under Covariate-shift Effects), a framework that decomposes $|ΔR|$ into an interpretable upper bound. This decomposition disentangles the risk change into four actionable factors: two generalization gaps, a model change penalty, and a covariate shift penalty, transforming the bound into a powerful diagnostic tool for understanding why performance has changed. To make TRACE a fully computable diagnostic, we instantiate each term. The covariate shift penalty is estimated via a model sensitivity factor (from high-quantile input gradients) and a data-shift measure; we use feature-space Optimal Transport (OT) by default and provide a robust alternative using Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD). The model change penalty is controlled by the average output distance between the two models on the target sample. Generalization gaps are estimated on held-out data. We validate our framework in an idealized linear regression setting, showing the TRACE bound correctly captures the scaling of the true risk difference with the magnitude of the shift. Across synthetic and vision benchmarks, TRACE diagnostics are valid and maintain a strong monotonic relationship with the true performance degradation. Crucially, we derive a deployment gate score that correlates strongly with $|ΔR|$ and achieves high AUROC/AUPRC for gating decisions, enabling safe, label-efficient model replacement.

</details>


### [150] [Towards Autonomous Mathematics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10177)
*Tony Feng,Trieu H. Trinh,Garrett Bingham,Dawsen Hwang,Yuri Chervonyi,Junehyuk Jung,Joonkyung Lee,Carlo Pagano,Sang-hyun Kim,Federico Pasqualotto,Sergei Gukov,Jonathan N. Lee,Junsu Kim,Kaiying Hou,Golnaz Ghiasi,Yi Tay,YaGuang Li,Chenkai Kuang,Yuan Liu,Hanzhao,Lin,Evan Zheran Liu,Nigamaa Nayakanti,Xiaomeng Yang,Heng-tze Cheng,Demis Hassabis,Koray Kavukcuoglu,Quoc V. Le,Thang Luong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Aletheia是一个数学研究智能体，能够从奥赛问题到博士级练习乃至研究论文生成，实现端到端的自然语言证明生成、验证和修订，展示了AI在数学研究中的里程碑成就。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型已在国际数学奥林匹克竞赛中达到金牌标准，但从竞赛级问题解决过渡到专业研究需要处理大量文献并构建长视野证明。本研究旨在开发能够自主进行数学研究的智能体，弥合这一差距。

Method: Aletheia采用迭代生成、验证和修订解决方案的端到端自然语言处理框架，由改进的Gemini Deep Think处理复杂推理问题，结合新颖的推理时缩放定律扩展至奥赛级以上问题，并密集使用工具应对数学研究的复杂性。

Result: Aletheia展示了从奥赛问题到博士级练习的能力，并实现了三个AI辅助数学研究的里程碑：(1)完全由AI生成的研究论文(Feng26)计算算术几何中的特征权重；(2)人机协作论文(LeeSeo26)证明独立集系统的边界；(3)对Bloom's Erdos Conjectures数据库中700个开放问题的半自主评估，包括自主解决四个开放问题。

Conclusion: 研究提出了量化AI辅助结果自主性和新颖性的标准等级，以帮助公众理解AI与数学的发展，并反思人机协作在数学研究中的未来前景。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundational models have yielded reasoning systems capable of achieving a gold-medal standard at the International Mathematical Olympiad. The transition from competition-level problem-solving to professional research, however, requires navigating vast literature and constructing long-horizon proofs. In this work, we introduce Aletheia, a math research agent that iteratively generates, verifies, and revises solutions end-to-end in natural language. Specifically, Aletheia is powered by an advanced version of Gemini Deep Think for challenging reasoning problems, a novel inference-time scaling law that extends beyond Olympiad-level problems, and intensive tool use to navigate the complexities of mathematical research.
